Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了“覆盖游戏”（Coverage Games），这是一种解决多智能体在对抗环境下如何分配并完成多个目标的博弈论框架，并对其理论属性及计算复杂度进行了深入探讨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多目标双人博弈框架难以处理系统对多智能体控制权有限，或需要动态分解目标以应对对抗性环境的场景（如多机器人巡检、资源耗尽防御）。

Method: 定义了覆盖方与干扰方之间的零和博弈规则，研究了博弈的确定性（Determinacy）、目标的先验分解能力，并对获胜判定的计算复杂度进行了全面分析。

Result: 确定了盖方/干扰方获胜判定问题的紧致复杂度（Tight Complexity），并探讨了单人博弈、固定智能体数量或固定目标数量等特殊情况下的性能表现。

Conclusion: 覆盖游戏为多智能体系统的安全性、健壮性分析和规划提供了一个强大的理论框架，揭示了目标分解策略在对抗环境中的重要性。

Abstract: We introduce and study coverage games - a novel framework for multi-agent planning in settings in which a system operates several agents but does not have full control on them, or interacts with an environment that consists of several agents. The game is played between a coverer, who has a set of objectives, and a disruptor. The coverer operates several agents that interact with the adversarial disruptor. The coverer wins if every objective is satisfied by at least one agent. Otherwise, the disruptor wins.
  Coverage games thus extend traditional two-player games with multiple objectives by allowing a (possibly dynamic) decomposition of the objectives among the different agents. They have many applications, both in settings where the system is the coverer (e.g., multi-robot surveillance, coverage in multi-threaded systems) and settings where it is the disruptor (e.g., prevention of resource exhaustion, ensuring non-congestion).
  We first study the theoretical properties of coverage games, including determinacy, and the ability to a priori decompose the objectives among the agents. We then study the problems of deciding whether the coverer or the disruptor wins. Besides a comprehensive analysis of the tight complexity of the problems, we consider interesting special cases, such as the one-player cases and settings with a fixed number of agents or objectives.

</details>


### [297] [Meta-Learning for Repeated Bayesian Persuasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20408)
*Ata Poyraz Turna,Asrin Efe Yorulmaz,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文首次将元学习引入贝叶斯劝说领域，提出了在任务相似时能显著提升劝说效率并在任意环境下保持鲁棒性的Meta-Persuasion算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的贝叶斯劝说通常关注单次交互，但在现实中交互往往是重复发生的。本文旨在通过元学习（Meta-learning）利用多个博弈任务之间的结构相似性来优化信号策略。

Method: 提出了Meta-Persuasion算法，涵盖了全反馈和老虎机反馈（Bandit-feedback）设置，并将其应用于在线贝叶斯劝说（OBP）和马尔可夫劝说过程（MPP）。

Result: 在任务相似的条件下，该算法实现了比现有OBP和MPP更优（更小）的遗憾率（Regret rates）；若任务是任意的，则退回到标准的单博弈性能保证。

Conclusion: Meta-Persuasion算法提供了一种灵活且高效的框架，既能利用任务相似性加速学习，又能在最坏情况下保持单任务的性能保障。

Abstract: Classical Bayesian persuasion studies how a sender influences receivers through carefully designed signaling policies within a single strategic interaction. In many real-world environments, such interactions are repeated across multiple games, creating opportunities to exploit structural similarity across tasks. In this work, we introduce Meta-Persuasion algorithms, establishing the first line of theoretical results for both full-feedback and bandit-feedback settings in the Online Bayesian Persuasion (OBP) and Markov Persuasion Process (MPP) frameworks. We show that our proposed meta-persuasion algorithms achieve provably sharper regret rates under natural notions of task similarity, improving upon the best-known convergence rates for both OBP and MPP. At the same time, they recover the standard single-game guarantees when the sequence of games is picked arbitrarily. Finally, we complement our theoretical analysis with numerical experiments that highlight our regret improvements and the benefits of meta-learning in repeated persuasion environments.

</details>


### [298] [Evolutionary Dynamics of Variable Games in Structured Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20603)
*Bin Pi,Minyu Feng,Liang-Jian Deng,Xiaojie Chen,Attila Szolnoki*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构化种群中的变量博弈框架，通过理论推导与优化模型，揭示了动态博弈环境如何影响合作演变，并找到了促进合作的最优博弈分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然界中的博弈互动具有不确定性和动态演化特征，但如何在结构化种群中有效刻画这些动态属性并确定促进合作的最优博弈分布仍是一项挑战。

Method: 引入变量博弈框架，利用马尔可夫链和对近似方法（pair approximation）推导自然选择倾向于合作的理论条件，并通过求解最优化问题确定最佳博弈分布。

Result: 推导出了弱选择下合作获胜的理论阈值，确定了能最大化合作选择梯度的最优博弈分布，且理论预测得到了数值计算和蒙特卡洛模拟的验证。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解复杂系统中的合作驱动机制提供了新见解，并为设计促进合作演化的高效博弈环境提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The game interactions among individuals in nature are often uncertain and dynamically evolving, significantly influencing the persistence of cooperation. However, it remains a formidable challenge to effectively characterize these dynamic properties in structured populations, derive theoretical conditions for cooperation, and identify the optimal game distribution for promoting cooperation. To address these issues, we propose the variable game framework in a structured population, where the game interactions between different individuals change over time. By means of the Markov chain and the pair approximation method, we derive theoretical conditions under which cooperation is favored by natural selection and when it is favored over defection under weak selection. Furthermore, we respectively formulate and solve two optimization problems to determine the optimal game distribution that most effectively fosters the evolution of cooperation by maximizing the gradient of cooperation selection and minimizing the fitness difference between defectors and cooperators. The theoretical predictions regarding both the conditions for cooperation and optimal game distribution are further validated by numerical calculations and extensive Monte Carlo simulations. Our findings offer novel insights into the mechanisms driving cooperative behavior in complex systems and provide theoretical guidance for designing optimal game environments that facilitate the evolution of cooperation.

</details>


### [299] [The survival of the weakest in a biased donation game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20998)
*Chaoqian Wang,Jingyang Li,Xinwei Wang,Wenqiang Zhu,Attila Szolnoki*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究发现，在社会博弈中，带有特定偏置的弱势“以牙还牙”策略能通过一种反直觉的“最弱者生存”机制，在结构化种群中最终胜过强势策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨“以牙还牙（Tit-for-Tat）”策略在社交困境中是否等同于无条件合作者，并研究策略内部偏置如何影响合作的演化动力学。

Method: 在捐赠博弈框架下引入带有独立偏置参数的“偏置以牙还牙（T）”策略，研究其与无条件合作者（C）及背叛者之间的互动，利用统计物理方法分析不同参数下的相图。

Result: 发现在结构化种群中存在“隐藏的T相”，即当T策略在适应度上处于劣势时，反而能通过抑制自身与C的关系并消除循环克制集群，最终主导整个人群。

Conclusion: 偏置调节在维持合作演化中起着微妙作用，揭示了“最弱者生存”效应在社会博弈中的普遍性。

Abstract: Cooperating first then mimicking the partner's act has been proven to be effective in utilizing reciprocity in social dilemmas. However, the extent to which this, called Tit-for-Tat strategy, should be regarded as equivalent to unconditional cooperators remains controversial. Here, we introduce a biased Tit-for-Tat (T) strategy that cooperates differently toward unconditional cooperators (C) and fellow T players through independent bias parameters. The results show that, even under strong dilemmas in the donation game framework, this three-strategy system can exhibit diverse phase diagrams on the parameter plane. In particular, when T-bias is small and C-bias is large, a ``hidden T phase'' emerges, in which the weakest T strategy dominates. The dominance of the weakened T strategy originates from a counterintuitive mechanism characterizing non-transitive ecological systems: T suppresses its relative fitness to C, rapidly eliminates the cyclic dominance clusters, and subsequently expands slowly to take over the entire population. Analysis in well-mixed populations confirms that this phenomenon arises from structured populations. Our study thus reveals the subtle role of bias regulation in cooperative modes by emphasizing the ``survival of the weakest'' effect in a broader context.

</details>


### [300] [Non-Exclusive Notifications for Ride-Hailing at Lyft II: Simulations and Marketplace Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21531)
*Farbod Ekbatani,Rad Niazadeh,Mehdi Golari,Romain Camilleri,Titouan Jehl,Chris Sholley,Matthew Leventi,Theresa Calderon,Angela Lam,Paul Havard Duclos,Tim Holland,James Koch,Shreya Reddy*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了打车平台从“一对一派单”转向“群发派单（NED）”的影响，证明 NED 能显著提升匹配效率，并探讨了不同冲突解决策略对系统长期表现的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 打车平台面临司机接单意向不确定的挑战。传统的“一对一”派单（ED）在司机拒绝或响应超时时需重新派单，导致乘客等待时间长、市场效率低。而非独占派单（NED）虽能降低延迟，但在通知集合选择和司机冲突处理上面临挑战。

Method: 采用了三位一体的研究方法：1) 建立受限福利最大化优化模型；2) 基于 Lyft 真实数据的轨迹进行大规模离散事件仿真；3) 构建简化的宏观均衡模型进行理论分析。

Result: NED 显著改善了核心履约指标，减少了匹配时间（降低了乘客取消率），同时增加了成交量和平均匹配质量。研究还量化了“先到先得”与“择优录取”两种机制在速度与质量之间的权衡。

Conclusion: NED 优于传统的 ED 模式，能有效降低匹配延迟并提升市场整体福利。在冲突解决机制上，First-Accept 侧重速度，而 Best-Accept 侧重匹配质量。此外，适当控制通知范围有助于维持长期的系统可用性。

Abstract: Ride-hailing platforms increasingly face uncertain driver acceptance, which makes traditional one-to-one 'exclusive dispatch (ED)' less efficient: rejections and timeouts force sequential retries and lengthen rider wait times, which in turn creates friction in the marketplace. 'Non-exclusive dispatch (NED)' mitigates this friction by broadcasting a request to multiple drivers in parallel. While NED can reduce latency, it introduces new design challenges -- most notably, how to choose notification sets and how to resolve driver contention (when multiple drivers accept the same ride).
  In this paper -- the second in a two-part collaboration with Lyft -- we develop a theoretically grounded framework to evaluate the long-run performance and marketplace effects of transitioning from ED to NED. We bridge theory and practice by combining (i) an optimization model that formulates NED as a constrained welfare maximization problem with (ii) large-scale discrete-event simulations on proprietary Lyft traces and (iii) a stylized macroscopic equilibrium model. Across simulation and equilibrium analysis, we find that NED improves key fulfillment metrics relative to ED: it reduces match time (and hence rider reneging) while increasing both the number and the average quality of completed matches. We also quantify the speed--quality trade-off between two common contention resolution rules, 'First-Accept' and 'Best-Accept': First-Accept maximizes speed and throughput, whereas Best-Accept is required to maximize per-match quality. Finally, we show that slightly conservative notification heuristics can improve long-run efficiency by avoiding excessive locking of high-value drivers and preserving future availability.

</details>


### [301] [Stationary Online Contention Resolution Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21532)
*Mohammad Reza Aminian,Rad Niazadeh,Pranav Nuti*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最大熵的平稳在线竞争分辩方案（S-OCRS）框架，将复杂的在线资源分配算法简化为概率分布构建问题，在多个经典场景下实现了最优或改进的性能保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 OCRS 设计通常依赖于复杂的间接约简（如先知不等式或 LP 对偶），导致算法难以理解或实现，缺乏直观的设计框架。

Method: 引入“平稳在线竞争分辩方案（S-OCRSs）”，这是一类具有排列不变性的 OCRS。通过“最大熵”方法将在线策略设计转化为构建合适的集合分布，并提供了一种通用的在线实现机制。

Result: 在二分匹配中获得了 $(3-\sqrt{5})/2$ 的选择率（已知最优）；在 k-均匀拟阵中获得 $1-\sqrt{2/(\pi k)}+O(1/k)$ 的选择率；在弱 Rayleigh 拟阵中获得简洁显式的 $1/2$ 选择率。

Conclusion: S-OCRS 框架简化了在线算法设计，不仅在多种经典可行性环境下（如二分匹配、k-均匀拟阵等）达到了或超越了现有最优界限，还提供了更具解释性和易于实现的算法构造。

Abstract: Online contention resolution schemes (OCRSs) are a central tool in Bayesian online selection and resource allocation: they convert fractional ex-ante relaxations into feasible online policies while preserving each marginal probability up to a constant factor. Despite their importance, designing (near) optimal OCRSs is often technically challenging, and many existing constructions rely on indirect reductions to prophet inequalities and LP duality, resulting in algorithms that are difficult to interpret or implement.
  In this paper, we introduce "stationary online contention resolution schemes (S-OCRSs)," a permutation-invariant class of OCRSs in which the distribution of the selected feasible set is independent of arrival order. We show that S-OCRSs admit an exact distributional characterization together with a universal online implementation. We then develop a general `maximum-entropy' approach to construct and analyze S-OCRSs, reducing the design of online policies to constructing suitable distributions over feasible sets. This yields a new technical framework for designing simple and possibly improved OCRSs. We demonstrate the power of this framework across several canonical feasibility environments. In particular, we obtain an improved $(3-\sqrt{5})/2$-selectable OCRS for bipartite matchings, attaining the independence benchmark conjectured to be optimal and yielding the best known prophet inequality for this setting. We also obtain a $1-\sqrt{2/(πk)} + O(1/k)$-selectable OCRS for $k$-uniform matroids and a simple, explicit $1/2$-selectable OCRS for weakly Rayleigh matroids (including all $\mathbb{C}$-representable matroids such as graphic and laminar). While these guarantees match the best known bounds, our framework also yields concrete and systematic constructions, providing transparent algorithms in settings where previous OCRSs were implicit or technically involved.

</details>


### [302] [Prophets Inequalities with Uncertain Acceptance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21740)
*Martinez Emile,Garrido-Lucero Felipe,Grandi Umberto,Pérez-Salazar Sebastian*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种带“不确定接受”的先知不等式模型，证明了其基本竞争比为1/2，并揭示了掌握“数值信息”比掌握“接受结果”对提升决策效率更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在传统的先知不等式模型基础上，引入“尝试选择可能失败”的不确定性，研究决策者在不同信息水平下（仅知分布 vs 已知价值 vs 已知接受结果）的竞争比。

Method: 将“价值感知的决策者”问题规约为带有缩放伯努利分布的经典先知不等式问题，并通过一系列变换将其简化为单一的最优化问题进行分析。

Result: 证明了在多种基准下（包括面对全知先知时）的最坏情况竞争比均为1/2；同时给出了在何种充分条件下，仅感知价值的决策者能超越1/2的性能屏障。

Conclusion: 在不确定接受率的先知不等式模型中，决策者获得价值信息的价值高于获得接受结果信息的价值，且在特定条件下能打破1/2的性能边界。

Abstract: We introduce the \textit{prophet inequality with uncertain acceptance} model, in which a decision maker sequentially observes a sequence of independent options, each characterized by a value $x_i$ and an acceptance probability $p_i$, both sampled from a known joint distribution. At time $i$, the decision maker observes the value $x_i$ and must irrevocably and immediately decide whether to attempt to select it or to continue to the next time step. If the option is selected, the process terminates with probability $p_i$ and the decision maker obtains $x_i$; otherwise, she continues searching.
  In this setting, two natural benchmarks arise: the \textit{value-aware decision-maker}, who knows all value realizations in advance but not the acceptance outcomes, and the \textit{full-knowledge prophet}, who knows all realizations beforehand and can choose the best option among those that will be accepted.
  We characterize the worst-case competitive ratios between our defined agents and show that all these values equal $1/2$. In addition, we provide sufficient conditions under which the value-aware decision-maker surpasses the $1/2$-barrier against the more informed prophet. This demonstrates the (crucial) interest for the decision maker to improve her knowledge over the values rather than over the acceptances, and is obtained via a more general result that reduces the value-aware decision-maker's problem to a classical prophet inequality with scaled Bernoulli distributions, followed by a sequence of transformations that further reduce the problem to a unique optimization problem.

</details>


### [303] [Individual Rationality in Constrained Hedonic Games: Additively Separable and Fractional Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21826)
*Foivos Fioravantes,Harmender Gahlawat,Nikolaos Melissinos,Šimon Schierreich*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在联盟数量及规模受限的享乐博弈中，寻找满足个体理性（IR）分配的计算复杂性，证明了该问题在不同偏好图结构下的难易程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在享乐博弈中，虽然个体理性（IR）通常易于满足，但在必须形成固定数量 $k$ 且具有规模限制（上下界）的联盟时，判别 IR 分配是否存在变得极具挑战。

Method: 通过分析偏好图（preference graph）的结构特征，结合加性可分（additively separable）和分数（fractional）享乐博弈模型，进行算法设计与硬度证明。

Result: 揭示了该问题的计算复杂性景观：发现即使在受限模型下，实现可解性也需要非常复杂的理论论证；同时通过配套的不可解性结果，完整界定了问题的难易界限。

Conclusion: 该研究描绘了在满足特定联盟数量和规模约束下，寻找IR分配的计算复杂性全景图，表明约束条件的引入显著提升了原本简单问题的复杂度。

Abstract: Hedonic games are an archetypal problem in coalition formation, where a set of selfish agents want to partition themselves into stable coalitions. In this work, we focus on two natural constraints on the possible outcomes. First, we require that exactly k coalitions are created. Then, loosely following the model of Bilò et al. (AAAI 2022), we assume that each of the k coalitions is additionally associated with a lower and upper bound on its size. The notion of stability that we study is that of individual rationality (IR), which requires that no agent strictly prefers to be alone compared to being in his or her coalition.
  Although IR is trivially satisfiable even in the most general models of hedonic games, the complexity picture of deciding whether an IR allocation exists, considering the above constraints, is unexpectedly rich. We reveal that tractable fragments of this computational problem require surprisingly nontrivial arguments, even if we restrict ourselves to additively separable and fractional hedonic games. Our tractability results, achieved by exploiting the structure of the underlying preference graph, are also complemented by their intractability counterparts, painting a fairly complete picture of the tractability landscape of this problem.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [304] [From Attention to Dialogue: Does Audience Engagement Reinforce Constructive Cross-Party Communication?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20549)
*Ahana Biswas,Yu-Ru Lin*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 研究发现社交媒体受众的反馈会积极塑造政治精英的行为，促使他们进行更多且更具建设性的跨党派对话，而非仅仅加剧极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨精英与受众之间是否存在反向影响，即社交媒体上的受众反应是否会塑造政治精英的跨党派互动（CPI）行为。

Method: 分析了2020-2021年间美国州立法者及其受众在Twitter/X上的超过110万条跨党派转发、回复和提信数据。

Result: 受众参与会产生反馈循环：立法者在经历高关注度的跨党派互动后，未来更有可能再次参与此类互动，并在修辞上转向因果推理、主观表达和积极情绪框架。

Conclusion: 研究挑战了“社交媒体仅加剧极化”的简化观点，表明受众参与可以与政治精英产生正向反馈回路，推动建设性的跨党派交流。

Abstract: While existing works have emphasized how elites shape mass opinion, we ask whether the reverse also holds: do audience reactions on social media actively shape elite behavior? We examine this question through the lens of cross-partisan interactions (CPIs), which can either foster deliberation or deepen polarization. Using a dataset of over 1.1 million cross-party retweets, replies, and mentions between U.S. state legislators and their audiences on Twitter/X (2020-2021), we first establish baseline patterns of engagement: Democrats gain modest engagement in replies and mentions, while Republicans often face penalties in direct cross-party interactions. Building on this, we show that audience engagement produces a feedback loop that conditions future elite behavior. Following highly visible CPIs, legislators are not only more likely to engage again in cross-talk, but also shift their rhetorical strategies. Engagement consistently promotes causal reasoning, subjective language, and positive-emotion framing in subsequent CPIs. These findings suggest a positive association between audience engagement and constructive cross-party discourse among elites, challenging overly simplified interpretations in the literature that emphasize social media as a primary driver of rising or falling polarization.

</details>


### [305] [negMIX: Negative Mixup for OOD Generalization in Open-Set Node Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20798)
*Junwei Gong,Xiao Shen,Zhihao Chen,Shirui Pan,Xiao Wang,Xi Zhou*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出 negMIX 模型，通过负采样混合（Negative Mixup）和跨层图对比学习，解决了开放集节点分类中的 OOD 泛化及类别表征学习难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开放集节点分类（OSNC）研究在增强对分布外（OOD）节点的泛化能力，以及促进类别间的紧凑性与分离度方面仍存在挑战。

Method: 提出了 negMIX 模型，包含两个核心组件：1. 专门为开放集设计的 Negative Mixup 方法，用于模拟 OOD 节点并强化决策边界；2. 跨层图对比学习模块，通过最大化不同拓扑距离邻域内同类节点的原型互信息，增强类内紧凑性和类间可分离性。

Result: 在多种实验场景和设定下，negMIX 的表现显著优于现有的最先进（SOTA）方法。

Conclusion: negMIX 是一种在开放集节点分类任务中平衡泛化性与判别力的优越方案，为处理图数据中的未知类别提供了新思路。

Abstract: Open-set node classification (OSNC) allows unlabeled test data to contain novel classes previously unseen in the labeled data. The goal is to classify in-distribution (ID) nodes into corresponding known classes and reject out-of-distribution (OOD) nodes as unknown class. Despite recent notable progress in OSNC, two challenges remain less explored, i.e., how to enhance generalization to OOD nodes, and promote intra-class compactness and inter-class separability. To tackle such challenges, we propose a novel Negative Mixup with Cross-Layer Graph Contrastive Learning (negMIX) model. Firstly, we devise a novel negative Mixup method purposefully crafted for the open-set scenario with theoretical justification, to enhance the model's generalization to OOD nodes and yield clearer ID/OOD boundary. Additionally, a unique cross-layer graph contrastive learning module is developed to maximize the prototypical mutual information between the same class nodes across different topological distance neighborhoods, thereby facilitating intra-class compactness and inter-class separability. Extensive experiments validate significant outperformance of the proposed negMIX over state-of-the-art methods in various scenarios and settings.

</details>


### [306] [Learning Inflation Narratives from Reddit: How Lightweight LLMs Reveal Forward-Looking Economic Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21501)
*Ryuichi Saito,Sho Tsugawa*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究通过微调轻量级大语言模型分析Reddit十年数据，构建了通胀感知指数（RIS），发现该指数不仅与真实经济指标高度相关，且具有领先预测价值，能揭示传统指标无法捕捉的各行业叙事动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的公众通胀预期衡量方式（如调查问卷）频率低、维度单一，且缺乏对底层叙事和特定行业关注点的深入理解。

Method: 使用针对特定领域（Reddit社区）微调的轻量级大语言模型（LLMs），针对美国CPI构成要素对帖子进行分类，生成月度Reddit通胀得分（RIS）。

Result: 1. RIS与CPI（r=0.91）及密歇根大学通胀预期高度相关；2. 格兰杰因果检验显示其具有领先于CPI和MICH的预测能力；3. 揭示了食品、交通、住房等行业的叙事转变。

Conclusion: 基于社交媒体的NLP方法能够作为传统宏观经济指标的有力补充，在政策制定和通胀压力预警中发挥重要作用。

Abstract: Public perceptions and expectations of inflation shape household spending, wage bargaining, and policy support, making them key determinants of macroeconomic outcomes. However, current measures rely on infrequent surveys and offer limited insight into underlying narratives and sector-specific concerns. This paper presents a novel approach to measuring public perception of inflation, using lightweight large language models (LLMs) fine-tuned on domain-specific Reddit data. We created an inflation classifier trained on posts related to components of the U.S. Consumer Price Index (CPI). When applied to more than 10 years of Reddit discussions (2012-2022), this classifier produces monthly Reddit inflation scores (RIS), which we validated against actual economic indicators. Our results show that fine-tuned lightweight LLMs perform well even with smaller training datasets, and the Reddit inflation scores strongly correlate with CPI (r=0.91) and closely align with the University of Michigan: Inflation Expectation (MICH). Importantly, Granger causality tests suggested that social media-based inflation scores often precede movements in both CPI and MICH, indicating their potential as predictive, forward-looking economic signals. Furthermore, change-point and lexical analyses uncovered shifts in inflation-related narratives across sectors like groceries, transportation, and housing, revealing dimensions of inflation concern that are not directly observable in aggregate price indices. By complementing traditional economic indicators with narrative-rich signals, this study demonstrates how NLP-based measures can facilitate earlier detection of inflationary pressures and policy responses.

</details>


### [307] [Asymmetric Dynamics of Partisan Warriors in YouTube Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21776)
*Keyeun Lee,Sang Jung Kim*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 研究通过分析YouTube评论提出了“党派武士”概念，发现跨界交流常伴随攻击性，且保守派环境中对他者的非文明攻击更容易获得受众奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管跨界评论被视为审议的途径，但现实中常引发敌意。本研究旨在通过提出“党派武士（partisan warriors）”概念，解释为何跨意识形态交流会演变为非文明攻击。

Method: 分析了2024年美国第二次总统辩论期间的1,854,320条YouTube评论。利用大语言模型（LLM）识别攻击目标，并对评论者的毒性和跨意识形态参与行为进行量化。

Result: 1. 跨界评论者并不比圈内评论者更文明；2. 保守派受众倾向于奖励对异见领袖的攻击，而自由派则不然；3. 保守派频道中的党派武士更普遍；4. 这种行为更多由频道环境而非单个视频标题触发。

Conclusion: 党派武士行为不仅是个人行为，更是一种生态现象，由频道层面的异质性和特定的受众激励机制共同驱动，深化了对社交媒体极化动态的理解。

Abstract: Cross-cutting commenting on social media is often imagined as a path to deliberation, yet exposure to opposing views frequently fuels hostility. To explain this dynamic, we introduce the concept of partisan warriors--commenters who cross ideological lines primarily to launch uncivil attacks against out-partisans. We analyze a large corpus of YouTube comments (N= 1,854,320) surrounding the 2024 U.S. second presidential debate. After filtering for toxicity and active participation, we use large language models to identify attack targets and operationalize partisan warrior behavior. Our analysis highlights four dynamics. First, cross-cutting commenters do not exhibit greater civility than those who remain within their ideological camps (RQ1). Second, audience reactions diverge by ideology: conservative audiences tended to reward hostile attacks on out-group leaders, whereas liberal audiences offered no comparable incentives and at times penalized such attacks (RQ2). Third, partisan warriors are notably more prevalent in conservative-leaning channels than in liberal ones; commenters restricted to conservative spaces were substantially more likely to engage in partisan warrior behavior compared to their liberal-only counterparts (RQ3). Finally, regarding environmental triggers, robustness checks suggest that this participation is an ecological phenomenon driven largely by channel-level heterogeneity rather than transient responses to individual video titles (RQ4). By shifting attention from the prevalence of incivility to its targets, rewards, and structural drivers, this study advances understanding of how partisan hostility is enacted and sustained in online spaces.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [308] [Decorrelation, Diversity, and Emergent Intelligence: The Isomorphism Between Social Insect Colonies and Ensemble Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20328)
*Ernest Fokoué,Gregory Babbitt,Yuval Leventhal*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出并证明了蚁群决策与随机森林算法在数学上是对等的（同构），展示了生物群体和机器学习如何通过相同的方差缩减策略实现去中心化智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立自然界（蚁群决策）与计算领域（集成学习，特别是随机森林）之间去中心化信息处理的严谨数学联系。

Method: 通过贝叶斯推理、多臂老虎机理论和统计学习理论，推导蚂蚁招募率与树权重、费洛蒙强化与袋外误差估计、以及群体感应与预测平均之间的具体映射关系。

Result: 证明了蚁群决策与随机森林在“随机集成智能”形式下是同构的；蚂蚁的功能分化机制精确对应于随机森林中的自助聚合（bagging）和特征子采样。

Conclusion: 群体智能（无论是生物还是人工）都遵循一个通用原则：随机的同质个体结合多样性增强机制，能够产生涌现的全局最优性。

Abstract: Social insect colonies and ensemble machine learning methods represent two of the most successful examples of decentralized information processing in nature and computation respectively. Here we develop a rigorous mathematical framework demonstrating that ant colony decision-making and random forest learning are isomorphic under a common formalism of \textbf{stochastic ensemble intelligence}. We show that the mechanisms by which genetically identical ants achieve functional differentiation -- through stochastic response to local cues and positive feedback -- map precisely onto the bootstrap aggregation and random feature subsampling that decorrelate decision trees. Using tools from Bayesian inference, multi-armed bandit theory, and statistical learning theory, we prove that both systems implement identical variance reduction strategies through decorrelation of identical units. We derive explicit mappings between ant recruitment rates and tree weightings, pheromone trail reinforcement and out-of-bag error estimation, and quorum sensing and prediction averaging. This isomorphism suggests that collective intelligence, whether biological or artificial, emerges from a universal principle: \textbf{randomized identical agents + diversity-enforcing mechanisms $\rightarrow$ emergent optimality}.

</details>


### [309] [Forward and inverse problems for measure flows in Bayes Hilbert spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20329)
*S. David Mis,Maarten V. de Hoop*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了贝叶斯-希尔伯特空间中时变概率测度的正反问题，提出了最小能量流匹配解释，并建立了具有正则化保证的动态重构理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决贝叶斯-希尔伯特空间中随时间变化的概率度量的正向动力学实现及其从间接观测中进行逆向重构的理论挑战。

Method: 通过加权 Neumann 问题将对数密度变化转化为最小动能梯度速度场，并结合 Sobolev 正则化与可观测性假设，在路径空间构建变分反问题。

Result: 证明了最小化器的存在性，推导了一阶变分公式，确立了观测映射的局部稳定性，并实现了对演化规律、分数函数及其规范速度场的强拓扑恢复。

Conclusion: 该框架为处理贝叶斯-希尔伯特空间中的演化概率度量提供了严谨的数学基础，实现了动态路径与其速度场之间的确定性映射。

Abstract: We study forward and inverse problems for time-dependent probability measures in Bayes--Hilbert spaces. On the forward side, we show that each sufficiently regular Bayes--Hilbert path admits a canonical dynamical realization: a weighted Neumann problem transforms the log-density variation into the unique gradient velocity field of minimum kinetic energy. This construction induces a transport form on Bayes--Hilbert tangent directions, which measures the dynamical cost of realizing prescribed motions, and yields a flow-matching interpretation in which the canonical velocity field is the minimum-energy execution of the prescribed path.
  On the inverse side, we formulate reconstruction directly on Bayes--Hilbert path space from time-dependent indirect observations. The resulting variational problem combines a data-misfit term with the transport action induced by the forward geometry. In our infinite-dimensional setting, however, this transport geometry alone does not provide sufficient compactness, so we add explicit temporal and spatial regularization to close the theory. The linearized observation operator induces a complementary observability form, which quantifies how strongly tangent directions are seen through the data. Under explicit Sobolev regularity and observability assumptions, we prove existence of minimizers, derive first-variation formulas, establish local stability of the observation map, and deduce recovery of the evolving law, its score, and its canonical velocity field under the strong topologies furnished by the compactness theory.

</details>


### [310] [Operator Learning for Smoothing and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20359)
*Edoardo Calvello,Elizabeth Carlson,Nikola Kovachki,Michael N. Manta,Andrew M. Stuart*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过建立通用逼近定理，为基于神经算子的纯数据驱动动力系统平滑与预测方法提供了首个完整的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在动力系统数据同化和预测中表现出潜力，但与模型驱动算法相比，这类数据驱动方法的理论分析极度匮乏。

Method: 采用了神经算子（Neural Operator）架构，并在连续时间设定下，结合映射存在性分析与算子学习架构的逼近特性进行理论推导。

Result: 建立了首个针对动力系统平滑和预测的纯数据驱动算法的通用逼近定理（Universal Approximation Theorem），并在Lorenz '63、Lorenz '96和Kuramoto-Sivashinsky系统中得到了实验验证。

Conclusion: 本研究为纯数据驱动的动力系统平滑和预测奠定了坚实的理论基础，弥补了该领域与传统模型驱动方法之间的分析差距。

Abstract: Machine learning has opened new frontiers in purely data-driven algorithms for data assimilation in, and for forecasting of, dynamical systems; the resulting methods are showing some promise. However, in contrast to model-driven algorithms, analysis of these data-driven methods is poorly developed. In this paper we address this issue, developing a theory to underpin data-driven methods to solve smoothing problems arising in data assimilation and forecasting problems. The theoretical framework relies on two key components: (i) establishing the existence of the mapping to be learned; (ii) the properties of the operator learning architecture used to approximate this mapping. By studying these two components in conjunction, we establish the first universal approximation theorem for purely data-driven algorithms for both smoothing and forecasting of dynamical systems. We work in the continuous time setting, hence deploying neural operator architectures. The theoretical results are illustrated with experiments studying the Lorenz `63, Lorenz `96 and Kuramoto-Sivashinsky dynamical systems.

</details>


### [311] [Comprehensive Description of Uncertainty in Measurement for Representation and Propagation with Scalable Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20365)
*Ali Darijani,Jürgen Beyerer,Zahra Sadat Hajseyed Nasrollah,Luisa Hoffmann,Michael Heizmann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个基于高斯混合模型（GMM）的框架，旨在解决传统测量系统中单一高斯分布无法准确描述复杂不确定性的问题，实现了高精度且计算高效的不确定性传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学与工程（尤其是测量控制领域）过度依赖简单的单一高斯分布假设，导致在多阶段流程中无法准确表示和传播复杂的不确定性。

Method: 提出一种基于概率密度函数（PDFs）的框架，利用高斯混合模型（GMMs）作为通用近似器，并结合数学抽象与计算优化，实现测量系统中属性的闭式解运算。

Result: 在循环工厂等制造和测量场景的应用表明，该框架比传统高斯框架更精确，且能根据硬件内存限制动态调整复杂度（权衡精度与计算量）。

Conclusion: 高斯混合模型（GMM）是测量和控制系统中传统高斯方法的理想替代方案，能在保持计算可处理性的同时，显著提升不确定性表达的完整性和传播的准确性。

Abstract: Probability theory has become the predominant framework for quantifying uncertainty across scientific and engineering disciplines, with a particular focus on measurement and control systems. However, the widespread reliance on simple Gaussian assumptions--particularly in control theory, manufacturing, and measurement systems--can result in incomplete representations and multistage lossy approximations of complex phenomena, including inaccurate propagation of uncertainty through multi stage processes.
  This work proposes a comprehensive yet computationally tractable framework for representing and propagating quantitative attributes arising in measurement systems using Probability Density Functions (PDFs). Recognizing the constraints imposed by finite memory in software systems, we advocate for the use of Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs), a principled extension of the familiar Gaussian framework, as they are universal approximators of PDFs whose complexity can be tuned to trade off approximation accuracy against memory and computation. From both mathematical and computational perspectives, GMMs enable high performance and, in many cases, closed form solutions of essential operations in control and measurement.
  The paper presents practical applications within manufacturing and measurement contexts especially circular factory, demonstrating how the GMMs framework supports accurate representation and propagation of measurement uncertainty and offers improved accuracy--compared to the traditional Gaussian framework--while keeping the computations tractable.

</details>


### [312] [Auto-differentiable data assimilation: Co-learning of states, dynamics, and filtering algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20891)
*Melissa Adrian,Daniel Sanz-Alonso,Rebecca Willett*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动微分滤波框架，通过梯度优化实现数据同化中状态预测、动力学学习与参数调优的自动化与集成化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据同化算法严重依赖高成本的参数调优和精确的动力学模型，难以处理模型不确定性或复杂参数配置的问题。

Method: 提出了“自动微分滤波”（Auto-differentiable filtering）框架，利用受理论启发的损失函数，通过自动微分和基于梯度的优化方法，从局部且含有噪声的观测数据中联合学习状态、动力学模型和算法参数。

Result: 在航空航天（Clohessy-Wiltshire）、大气科学（Lorenz-96）和系统生物学（Lotka-Volterra）等多个领域的实验证明，该框架具有极高的灵活性和通用性，能有效优化滤波性能。

Conclusion: Auto-differentiable filtering 提供了一个通用的框架，能够有效地将传统数据同化方法与现代自动微分技术相结合，适用于各科学领域的动态系统建模。

Abstract: Data assimilation algorithms estimate the state of a dynamical system from partial observations, where the successful performance of these algorithms hinges on costly parameter tuning and on employing an accurate model for the dynamics. This paper introduces a framework for jointly learning the state, dynamics, and parameters of filtering algorithms in data assimilation through a process we refer to as auto-differentiable filtering. The framework leverages a theoretically motivated loss function that enables learning from partial, noisy observations via gradient-based optimization using auto-differentiation. We further demonstrate how several well-known data assimilation methods can be learned or tuned within this framework. To underscore the versatility of auto-differentiable filtering, we perform experiments on dynamical systems spanning multiple scientific domains, such as the Clohessy-Wiltshire equations from aerospace engineering, the Lorenz-96 system from atmospheric science, and the generalized Lotka-Volterra equations from systems biology. Finally, we provide guidelines for practitioners to customize our framework according to their observation model, accuracy requirements, and computational budget.

</details>


### [313] [Stability of Sequential and Parallel Coordinate Ascent Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20929)
*Debdeep Pati*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对比了变分推理中顺序与并行坐标上升算法的行为，揭示了并行算法虽有效率优势但收敛稳定性逊于顺序算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然顺序与并行坐标上升算法的区别在简单数值分析中已知，但在变分推理领域，尤其是复杂模型下，这种行为差异及其理论影响尚未得到深入探索。

Method: 以中高维线性回归模型为切入点，针对坐坐标上升变分推理（CAVI）的两种核心变体——顺序算法（Sequential）和并行算法（Parallel）进行理论比较。

Result: 通过分析发现，顺序算法虽然计算速度通常较慢，但在更宽松的条件下仍能保证收敛；相比之下，旨在通过块更新提高效率的并行算法在收敛鲁棒性上表现较差。

Conclusion: 在复杂概率模型的变分推理中，算法的选择（顺序 vs 并行）不仅影响速度，更决定了算法能否在更广泛的条件下收敛。应当警惕并行化带来的不稳定性。

Abstract: We highlight a striking difference in behavior between two widely used variants of coordinate ascent variational inference: the sequential and parallel algorithms. While such differences were known in the numerical analysis literature in simpler settings, they remain largely unexplored in the optimization-focused literature on variational inference in more complex models. Focusing on the moderately high-dimensional linear regression problem, we show that the sequential algorithm, although typically slower, enjoys convergence guarantees under more relaxed conditions than the parallel variant, which is often employed to facilitate block-wise updates and improve computational efficiency.

</details>


### [314] [Hard labels sampled from sparse targets mislead rotation invariant algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20967)
*Avrajit Ghosh,Bin Yu,Manfred Warmuth,Peter Bartlett*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了在逻辑回归中，传统的旋转不变算法（如普通梯度下降）在处理稀疏目标时存在明显的性能缺陷，而采用非旋转不变的重参数化方法能大幅优化样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在硬标签（Hard labels）采样环境下，逻辑回归学习稀疏目标的效率问题，并探讨为什么传统的旋转不变算法在处理此类问题时表现不佳。

Method: 对比分析了旋转不变算法（如逻辑回归上的梯度下降）与非旋转不变算法。提出使用权重重参数化方法（将 $w_i$ 表示为 $u_i v_i$）来处理稀疏目标。

Result: 证明了旋转不变算法在处理 $s$-稀疏目标时具有 $Ω(\frac{d-1}{n})$ 的过剩风险（Excess risk），而简单的非旋转不变算法（如 $u_iv_i$ 参数化）能将风险降低至 $O(\frac{s \log d}{n})$。

Conclusion: 在稀疏逻辑回归任务中，算法的旋转不变性（Rotation-invariance）是导致性能低下的关键瓶颈。通过非旋转不变的重参数化（如 $w_i = u_i v_i$），可以显著提升稀疏目标的学习效率。

Abstract: One of the most common machine learning setups is logistic regression. In many classification models, including neural networks, the final prediction is obtained by applying a logistic link function to a linear score. In binary logistic regression, the feedback can be either soft labels, corresponding to the true conditional probability of the data (as in distillation), or sampled hard labels (taking values $\pm 1$). We point out a fundamental problem that arises even in a particularly favorable setting, where the goal is to learn a noise-free soft target of the form $σ(\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{w}^{\star})$. In the over-constrained case (i.e. the number of samples $n$ exceeds the input dimension $d$) with examples $(\mathbf{x}_i,σ(\mathbf{x}_i^{\top}\mathbf{w}^{\star}))$, it is sufficient to recover $\mathbf{w}^{\star}$ and hence achieve the Bayes risk. However, we prove that when the examples are labeled by hard labels $y_i$ sampled from the same conditional distribution $σ(\mathbf{x}_i^{\top}\mathbf{w}^{\star})$ and $\mathbf{w}^{\star}$ is $s$-sparse, then rotation-invariant algorithms are provably suboptimal: they incur an excess risk $Ω\!\left(\frac{d-1}{n}\right)$, while there are simple non-rotation invariant algorithms with excess risk $O(\frac{s\log d}{n})$. The simplest rotation invariant algorithm is gradient descent on the logistic loss (with early stopping). A simple non-rotation-invariant algorithm for sparse targets that achieves the above upper bounds uses gradient descent on the weights $u_i,v_i$, where now the linear weight $w_i$ is reparameterized as $u_iv_i$.

</details>


### [315] [Stochastic approximation in non-markovian environments revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21091)
*Vivek Shripad Borkar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非遍历、非马尔可夫随机逼近的数学框架，用于解析 Transformer 注意力机制和持续学习中依赖全局历史信息的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立一种解析框架，用以理解 Transformer 的注意力机制（Attention）和持续学习（Continual Learning），这两者在原理上都依赖于全局历史信息。

Method: 扩展了作者近期关于非马尔可夫环境下随机逼近（Stochastic Approximation）的研究，引入了非遍历性（Non-ergodic）驱动过程的处理。

Result: 提出了一种能够处理非马尔可夫且非遍历驱动随机过程的随机逼近分析理论。

Conclusion: 该分析框架为理解 Transformer 的长程依赖处理能力提供了坚实的数学理论基础。

Abstract: Based on some recent work of the author on stochastic approximation in non-markovian environments, the situation when the driving random process is non-ergodic in addition to being non-markovian is considered. Using this, we propose an analytic framework for understanding transformer based learning, specifically, the `attention' mechanism, and continual learning, both of which depend on the entire past in principle.

</details>


### [316] [Time-adaptive functional Gaussian Process regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21144)
*MD Ruiz-Medina,AE Madrid,A Torres-Signes,JM Angulo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于测度论和拉普拉斯特征分解的流形时空函数型高斯过程回归方法，并通过角谱截断实现了在大样本下的有效降维预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对流形上的时空随机场，提出一种新的函数型高斯过程回归方案，以解决流形数据上的函数预测和降维问题。

Method: 基于经验贝叶斯框架，利用Hilbert空间中的紧高斯测度，并通过Laplace-Beltrami算子的特征函数将测度转换为无限乘积形式；核心在于利用随时间变化的角谱进行维数削减。

Result: 通过模拟研究和合成数据应用，验证了所提回归预测器在流形等距变换不变性及计算效率方面的优势。

Conclusion: 该回归方法在流形数据处理中具有良好的有限样本和渐近性质，能够有效捕捉时空动态特征。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new formulation of functional Gaussian Process regression in manifolds, based on an Empirical Bayes approach, in the spatiotemporal random field context. We apply the machinery of tight Gaussian measures in separable Hilbert spaces, exploiting the invariance property of covariance kernels under the group of isometries of the manifold. The identification of these measures with infinite-product Gaussian measures is then obtained via the eigenfunctions of the Laplace-Beltrami operator on the manifold. The involved time-varying angular spectra constitute the key tool for dimension reduction in the implementation of this regression approach, adopting a suitable truncation scheme depending on the functional sample size. The simulation study and synthetic data application undertaken illustrate the finite sample and asymptotic properties of the proposed functional regression predictor.

</details>


### [317] [Domain Elastic Transform: Bayesian Function Registration for High-Dimensional Scientific Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21235)
*Osamu Hirose,Emanuele Rodola*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 DET 的无网格概率框架，统一了几何与功能对齐，专门用于解决空间转录组等高维、稀疏且不规则数据的非刚性配准难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在点集配准（几何）和图像配准（强度）之间存在鸿沟。对于空间转录组学等新兴科学数据，现有的处理方式要么牺牲分辨率进行体素化以适应图像工具，要么忽略功能信号仅使用几何工具。

Method: 提出域弹性变换（Domain Elastic Transform, DET），这是一个无网格的概率框架。该方法在贝叶斯框架下运行，将数据视为不规则域上的函数，并将域变形建模为受空间-特征联合似然引导的弹性运动。

Result: 在 MERFISH 数据上实现了 92% 的拓扑保持率（对比最优传输方法的 <5%），并成功配准了涉及大规模、复杂生长变化的全胚胎 Stereo-seq 图谱。

Conclusion: DET 突破了传统非刚性配准在点集与图像之间的二元对立，为生命科学中复杂的高维稀疏数据提供了一套严谨且通用的配准方案。

Abstract: Nonrigid registration is conventionally divided into point set registration, which aligns sparse geometries, and image registration, which aligns continuous intensity fields on regular grids. However, this dichotomy creates a critical bottleneck for emerging scientific data, such as spatial transcriptomics, where high-dimensional vector-valued functions, e.g., gene expression, are defined on irregular, sparse manifolds. Consequently, researchers currently face a forced choice: either sacrifice single-cell resolution via voxelization to utilize image-based tools, or ignore the critical functional signal to utilize geometric tools. To resolve this dilemma, we propose Domain Elastic Transform (DET), a grid-free probabilistic framework that unifies geometric and functional alignment. By treating data as functions on irregular domains, DET registers high-dimensional signals directly without binning. We formulate the problem within a rigorous Bayesian framework, modeling domain deformation as an elastic motion guided by a joint spatial-functional likelihood. The method is fully unsupervised and scalable, utilizing feature-sensitive downsampling to handle massive atlases. We demonstrate that DET achieves 92\% topological preservation on MERFISH data where state-of-the-art optimal transport methods struggle ($<$5\%), and successfully registers whole-embryo Stereo-seq atlases across developmental stages -- a task involving massive scale and complex nonrigid growth. The implementation of DET is available on {https://github.com/ohirose/bcpd} (since Mar, 2025).

</details>


### [318] [Accelerate Vector Diffusion Maps by Landmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21247)
*Sing-Yuan Yeh,Yi-An Wu,Hau-Tieng Wu,Mao-Pei Tsui*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了 LA-VDM，一种通过地标约束和两阶段归一化加速矢量扩散映射的算法，在保证数学收敛性的同时提升了处理复杂流形数据的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在加速基于图联络拉普拉斯（GCL）的矢量扩散映射（VDM）框架，该框架虽能捕捉复杂数据集中的成对连接关系，但计算成本可能较高。

Method: 提出 LA-VDM 算法，通过地标约束（landmark-constrained）加速 VDM 框架，并引入一种新型的两阶段归一化方法来处理数据和地标集中的非均匀采样密度。

Result: 证明了在具有标架丛结构的流形模型下，LA-VDM 能通过点云准确恢复平行移动，并在模拟数据集和图像处理任务中验证了其高效性。

Conclusion: LA-VDM 在理论上渐近收敛于联络拉普拉斯算子，并在非局部图像去噪等实际应用中表现出优越的性能和准确性。

Abstract: We propose a landmark-constrained algorithm, LA-VDM (Landmark Accelerated Vector Diffusion Maps), to accelerate the Vector Diffusion Maps (VDM) framework built upon the Graph Connection Laplacian (GCL), which captures pairwise connection relationships within complex datasets. LA-VDM introduces a novel two-stage normalization that effectively address nonuniform sampling densities in both the data and the landmark sets. Under a manifold model with the frame bundle structure, we show that we can accurately recover the parallel transport with landmark-constrained diffusion from a point cloud, and hence asymptotically LA-VDM converges to the connection Laplacian. The performance and accuracy of LA-VDM are demonstrated through experiments on simulated datasets and an application to nonlocal image denoising.

</details>


### [319] [Closed-form conditional diffusion models for data assimilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21291)
*Brianna Binder,Assad Oberai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带有解析解的条件扩散模型，利用核密度估计实现无需显式物理模型的黑盒数据同化，在非线性动力系统测试中优于传统滤波方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统数据同化方法（如 Kalman 滤波）在处理非高斯、非线性系统及黑盒模型时的局限性。

Method: 利用核密度估计（KDE）对状态与观测值的联合分布建模，推导出解析形式（Closed-form）的得分函数，进而实现条件扩散生成。

Result: 在 Lorenz-63 和 Lorenz-96 系统测试中，该方法在中等规模样本量下展现了比传统滤波器更精确的同化能力。

Conclusion: 该模型在非高斯、非线性及黑盒系统中表现优于传统的 Ensemble Kalman 和 Particle 滤波器。

Abstract: We propose closed-form conditional diffusion models for data assimilation. Diffusion models use data to learn the score function (defined as the gradient of the log-probability density of a data distribution), allowing them to generate new samples from the data distribution by reversing a noise injection process. While it is common to train neural networks to approximate the score function, we leverage the analytical tractability of the score function to assimilate the states of a system with measurements. To enable the efficient evaluation of the score function, we use kernel density estimation to model the joint distribution of the states and their corresponding measurements. The proposed approach also inherits the capability of conditional diffusion models of operating in black-box settings, i.e., the proposed data assimilation approach can accommodate systems and measurement processes without their explicit knowledge. The ability to accommodate black-box systems combined with the superior capabilities of diffusion models in approximating complex, non-Gaussian probability distributions means that the proposed approach offers advantages over many widely used filtering methods. We evaluate the proposed method on nonlinear data assimilation problems based on the Lorenz-63 and Lorenz-96 systems of moderate dimensionality and nonlinear measurement models. Results show the proposed approach outperforms the widely used ensemble Kalman and particle filters when small to moderate ensemble sizes are used.

</details>


### [320] [Generalized Discrete Diffusion from Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21342)
*Oussama Zekri,Théo Uscidda,Nicolas Boullé,Anna Korba*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 GDDS，一个支持任意噪声过程的通用离散扩散框架，通过快照潜变量显著提升了训练效率和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散扩散模型在处理大规模离散状态空间时，受限于特定的噪声过程，缺乏灵活性且训练效率不足，无法在大规模任务上完全超越自回归模型。

Method: 提出 GDDS 框架，利用均一化（Uniformization）实现快速任意噪声处理，并推导基于“快照潜变量”（Snapshot latents）而非全路径的证据下界（ELBO）进行高效训练。

Result: 实验表明，GDDS 在训练效率和生成质量上均优于现有的离散扩散方法，并且在同等规模的任务中首次击败了自回归模型。

Conclusion: GDDS 为大规模离散数据生成提供了一个统一且高效的概率框架，在性能上超越了现有离散扩散模型和自回归模型。

Abstract: We introduce Generalized Discrete Diffusion from Snapshots (GDDS), a unified framework for discrete diffusion modeling that supports arbitrary noising processes over large discrete state spaces. Our formulation encompasses all existing discrete diffusion approaches, while allowing significantly greater flexibility in the choice of corruption dynamics. The forward noising process relies on uniformization and enables fast arbitrary corruption. For the reverse process, we derive a simple evidence lower bound (ELBO) based on snapshot latents, instead of the entire noising path, that allows efficient training of standard generative modeling architectures with clear probabilistic interpretation. Our experiments on large-vocabulary discrete generation tasks suggest that the proposed framework outperforms existing discrete diffusion methods in terms of training efficiency and generation quality, and beats autoregressive models for the first time at this scale. We provide the code along with a blog post on the project page : \href{https://oussamazekri.fr/gdds}{https://oussamazekri.fr/gdds}.

</details>
